## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a strong, consistent identity (purple X motif, full-bleed photography, one typeface) and a clear route: vision, three trends, five themes, three phases, enablers, targets, governance, close. The repeated phase graphic (slides 15, 16, 20, 25) works as a progress marker, and slide 7's large stat callouts are easy to read. The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support. The deck leans on metaphor imagery that often adds mood rather than meaning, and the 'Ultra Hight Net Worth' typo on slides 7 and 8 is visible.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the phase-ribbon device with the active phase highlighted, the one-idea-per-slide headlines, and the clean five-column portfolio structure on slide 13.

**Most useful repair:** Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.

**What would change the score:** Replace decorative photos behind data-bearing slides with simple charts or tables for the targets on slides 21 and 24, and trim the many mood-only interstitial slides.

_Confidence medium. 34 of 34 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a forward-looking statements disclaimer, then sets out SCBX's vision to be the most admired regional financial technology group. Its mission is to make finance simple, accessible and affordable, with an ambition to reach a THB 1 trillion valuation. The middle sections examine three global trends that shape its investments: financial inclusion for underserved segments through digital lenders, the digital asset economy built on blockchain and Web 3.0, and green tech, framed as a USD 50 billion opportunity. The deck then presents a five-theme portfolio structure and a three-phase strategy running over five years. It also covers three Centers of Excellence and two growth accelerators, financial targets for digital lenders, and a Better Bank plan with cost-to-income and ROE goals. Regional expansion steps follow, along with governance and investment discipline. The closing slides list selected awards and summarize why SCBX is positioned to grow. Slides use short headline-style titles and simple diagrams for the three-phase plan and portfolio structure, and many repeat the phase graphic to show progress.

## Questions about this deck

**What is SCBX's vision and mission according to the SCBX Strategy Day deck?**

Slide 3 states the vision as to be the most admired regional financial technology group. Slide 4 gives the mission as to make finance simple, accessible and affordable for all through the power of technology and innovation.

**How many slides does the SCBX Strategy Day presentation have?**

The SCBX Strategy Day deck has 34 slides, starting with the title slide on slide 1 and ending with the closing slide on slide 34.

**What are the three phases of SCBX's strategy in its deck?**

Slide 15 sets out Phase 1 as Build core tech & data capabilities (1-2 years), Phase 2 as Deliver growth with scale and synergy (2-3 years), and Phase 3 as Monetization and exponential growth (3-5 years).

**What Better Bank targets does the SCBX deck give for cost-to-income and ROE?**

Slide 24 states a Low Teens ROE by 2025 and a Mid-to-high 30s cost-to-income by 2025 as goals within its Better Bank strategy.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Our investment purposes under / three key global trends
5

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph with a centred two-line title set in the middle-lower area of the slide (line one starts at about 46% height, line two at about 54%), both lines centred on the horizontal midpoint. The page number sits in the bottom-left corner at about 4% from the left edge and 95% from the top. No header, footer or logo band.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of planet Earth seen from orbit at night, with the curved horizon running from the left-centre across to the upper right. Subject: the Atlantic coast of Africa on the left, the Iberian Peninsula, France, Italy and the Mediterranean with glowing city lights clustered across Europe and North Africa. The sun is rising behind the upper-right limb, creating a bright white-gold glow and a faint lens-flare streak extending upward to the top edge. Lighting is backlit from the upper right; the night side is near-black with navy and slate tones, the atmosphere rim is pale blue-white. Dominant colours: black #050A14, deep navy #1B2333, slate grey #3A4252, with white and warm gold highlights near the sunrise. The image is not cropped in a visible way beyond filling the frame; no border, no visible overlay or tint apart from the natural darkness of the scene.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed night photograph of Earth, dominated by near-black navy. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif bold (Visby CF Bold), about 7.4% of slide height, centred, tight leading with the two lines roughly 8% of height apart. Page number is set in a small sans-serif (Calibri) at about 5.2% of slide height. No accent colours, no line or shape styling, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients added to the text. A full-slide white fill exists behind the photograph but is fully covered by the image.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Market sizing is paired with a segmented chart and large stat callouts, so the opportunity is visible at a glance.
- Review: The repeated phase graphic (slides 15, 16, 20, 25) works as a progress marker, and slide 7's large stat callouts are easy to read.
- Review: The deck leans on metaphor imagery that often adds mood rather than meaning, and the 'Ultra Hight Net Worth' typo on slides 7 and 8 is visible.
- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.

**Text**

We are ready to unlock / the new opportunities
Ultra Hight Net Worth
Middle & Affluent
Upper Mass
Mass
Lower Mass
55%
of Thailand households lack access to loans
84%
Have smartphones, making digital lending the best way to engage them
THB 1.5tn 1
Revenue pool for digital lending in Thailand (and at least 5x potential in ASEAN)
1 SCBX estimates based on Euromonitor data
7

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a 16:9 slide. A stacked-stones photograph fills the lower-left, from the left edge to about 59% of the width and from about 22% to the bottom. A text column on the right starts at about 70% of the width with three stacked statistic blocks. The title sits top-left at about 8% from the left edge and 9% from the top, left-aligned over two lines. The footnote is bottom-right and the page number is bottom-left at about 2% from the left edge.

**Visuals**

1. Photograph (left, 0.0-59.4% x, 22.0-100% y, 1257x835 px): a vertical stack of five smooth grey river stones balanced on each other, from a small top stone to a wide base stone, on a white background with a soft grey shadow under the base. Each stone has a pale off-white vein or streak. The composition is centred, full-height of the stack, with the base stone near the bottom edge. Lighting is soft and even from the upper left. Dominant colours are charcoal grey and off-white. The image is not boxed and has no overlay.
2. Graphic background (right, 52.9-100% x, 0-100% y, 904x1080 px): a large faded grey chevron/X-shaped watermark shape. It runs from the top edge toward the lower right, with a light grey gradient from about #F4F4F4 at the top to about #C8C8C8 at the bottom. It is full-bleed on the right and sits behind the text.
3. Five purple leader lines (about #6C2993, about 0.2% stroke) run horizontally from each stone label to its stone: Ultra Hight Net Worth at y 31.3% from x 18.5% to 31.3%; Middle & Affluent at y 38.7% from x 18.5% to 31.4%; Upper Mass at y 49.1% from x 18.5% to 31.2%; Mass at y 64.1% from x 18.5% to 31.2%; Lower Mass at y 81.1% from x 27.2% to 42.0%.
4. Two dotted curves (lavender about #A77FC4, dotted, about 0.4% stroke): curve one rises from about (27.7%, 59.1%) to about (70.0%, 23.8%), starting near the middle stone and ending just below the 55% figure. Curve two runs from about (37.1%, 89.6%) to about (70.8%, 88.7%), starting at the base stone and ending near the end of the revenue text. Both curves are slightly concave.
5. Superscript '1' after THB 1.5tn refers to the footnote.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a faded grey chevron watermark (gradient #F4F4F4 to #C8C8C8) on the right side. Clean white corporate slide. Title colour #535353 charcoal grey in VisbyCF Bold (geometric sans-serif, bold weight), with the title about 5.6% of slide height in cap height terms and body labels in VisbyCF Medium at about 3.7%. Statistic figures are VisbyCF ExtraBold in purple #6C2993 at about 7.4% of slide height. Descriptions are VisbyCF Regular or Bold in #535353 at about 3.0% of slide height, with bold used for key phrases such as 'lack', 'Have smartphones', 'Revenue pool for digital' and 'lending'. Footnote is VisbyCF Regular at 2.6%. Page number is HelveticaNeue at about 2.2% in #5E5E5E. Lines are thin purple leader strokes and dotted lavender curves. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients beyond the background watermark.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.
- Review: The deck leans on metaphor imagery that often adds mood rather than meaning, and the 'Ultra Hight Net Worth' typo on slides 7 and 8 is visible.
- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.

**Text**

We are set up to win with several digital lenders, each with / a distinct focus and new efficiency for mass markets / Secured / Unsecured / Ultra Hight Net Worth / Middle & Affluent / Luxury vehicles / hire purchase / Upper Mass / Mortgages, auto / hire purchase & / secured loans / Digital loans / Mass / Lower Mass / Personal loans & / credit card / Nano revolving loans / with AI/ML superpower / Auto title loan / Fully-automated micro / digital lender with / in-house tech / 8

**Layout**

Single-column centred composition on a white field. The two-line headline sits centred across the top (about 7.5% to 17% of height) with Secured and Unsecured column labels at about 23% height, left and right of centre. A central stack of five stones spans the middle, with labels to its right (tier names) and to the left and right (product logos and text), and four horizontal purple dotted rules divide the slide into tiers at about 27%, 36%, 65% and 95% height. The slide number 8 sits bottom-left at about 95% height, inside a left margin of about 2%.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a vertical stack of five smooth grey beach stones balanced one on another, each with a pale off-white band wrapped around it, from a small top stone (Ultra Hight Net Worth) down to a wide base stone (Lower Mass); the stack is centred, slightly cropped by nothing, sits on a faint grey ground shadow at its base, and is lit evenly from the front-left with neutral grey tones (dominant colours #3A3A3A to #9A9A9A, bands #E8E4DE). Behind the stack is a very large faint light-grey X-shaped graphic formed by two diagonal wedge shapes fading from about #E6E6E6 at the bottom to white at the top, with the X crossing near the top-centre of the stack. Two purple (#6C2993) thin leader lines run from the top two stones to their labels. Logos: ALPHA X (thin spaced grey capitals with a red X) left of the top tier; SCB square app icon (purple #4B2B82 rounded square with a yellow-orange crest and SCB letters) twice, once left at mid-height and once right at mid-height; autoX (dark blue lowercase auto with a yellow and red X) left at the base tier; CardX (teal-to-blue wordmark) right; monix (grey wordmark with a cyan circle) right; SCB ABACUS (red circle icon and dark blue SCB with red ABACUS) right at the base tier. Four purple dotted horizontal rules, about 0.3% of height thick, span about 3.7% to 96.3% of width.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a faint light-grey X-shaped gradient graphic (#E6E6E6 fading to white) behind the centre. White background with a faint grey X-shaped gradient graphic behind the centre. Headline in Visby CF Bold, sans-serif geometric, #535353, about 4.4% of slide height, centred. Section labels Secured and Unsecured in Visby CF Bold, #6C2993 purple, about 3.7% of height. Tier labels in Visby CF Medium #FFFFFF on the stones and #535353 on white, about 3.7% for tier names and 2.8% for descriptive text. Accents are purple #6C2993 for the dotted rules and leader lines. Dotted rules are evenly spaced purple dots, no solid lines. The SCB tiles are purple rounded squares with small radius. Slide number in HelveticaNeue regular #5E5E5E at about 2.2% of height. No shadows apart from the soft ground shadow under the stone stack.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Trend 2 Digital Asset Economy:
With the potential to replace
the traditional financial markets
9

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph fills the slide. The title block is three centred lines set in the right-centre of the slide, starting about 39% down from the top. The page number sits in the bottom-left corner at about 2% from the left edge. There is no header, footer or logo.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of stacked coins, seen at a low angle with shallow depth of field. The coins recede diagonally from upper-left to lower-right, with blurred coin edges at the lower right. The palette is warm sepia and grey: dark brown shadows, beige-to-cream highlights and a warm grey background at upper right. The photo is not cropped visibly and is not boxed. A dark tint covers the left half, and a translucent purple diagonal band runs from the bottom-left corner up toward the top centre. Two large dark triangular shapes sit at the top-left and bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Photograph of stacked coins, full-bleed, with a dark translucent overlay on the left half (base fill #FFFFFF beneath the image). Dark, photographic look with a purple accent band. The background is the coin photograph with a dark overlay on the left. Title is white bold geometric sans-serif, about 7.4% of slide height per line, with tight leading. The page number is white regular sans-serif, about 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour is purple #6C2993 at 30% opacity over the left half. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Text**

FUTURE OF FINANCE / WITH BLOCKCHAIN TECHNOLOGY / AND DIGITAL ASSETS BUSINESS / DISRUPTION / EVOLUTION / Initial phase / Web 3.0 with / blockchain enabler / • Web3.0 converging / business and financial / layer, allowing users to / directly interact and / exchange value virtually / like in the physical world / • Web3.0 token / accelerates user / adoption while capturing / ecosystem value for / token holders / Banking Services / • Future of banking services with / blockchain backbone / • Examples: CBDC, stablecoins, / cryptocurrencies, smart contracts / for supply chain finance / Capital Markets / • Blockchain enabled efficiency with / asset tokenization & smart contract / processing / • Examples: Investment tokens, asset- / backed tokens / 10

**Layout**

Three-line all-caps title sits top-left at about 7.9% from the left edge and 15% from the top, left-aligned. Three text columns sit below on a rising diagonal: Capital Markets bottom-left (x 7.1%, y 74%), Banking Services centre (x 47.3%, y 67%), and Web 3.0 with blockchain enabler right (x 77.7%, y 34.5%). A purple curved arrow and a white straight arrow run diagonally from bottom-left to upper-right behind the columns. The page number 10 sits bottom-left at about x 1.7%, y 94.7%. No footer, logo or confidentiality mark.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a motion-blurred dark ocean wave: a grey-black wave crest sweeps diagonally from lower-left to upper-right, with streaks of smoky mauve-grey sky above. Soft, low-key, diffuse light; dominant colours charcoal #2A2A2E and muted mauve-grey #8F8285. A translucent dark-brown tint (#291B1A at 30% opacity) covers the left 48.4% of the slide. Lighter translucent diagonal wedges appear at top-left. Two diagrammatic arrows: (1) a purple #9B3CA0 curved arc, about 4 px thick, starting at a filled purple circle (about x 7%, y 62%, about 2% wide) and rising to an arrowhead at about x 90%, y 11%, labelled DISRUPTION, rotated about -25 degrees; (2) a straight white arrow, about 5 px thick, starting at a filled white circle (about x 7%, y 68%, about 2% wide) and running to an arrowhead at about x 61%, y 60%, labelled EVOLUTION (translucent grey-white, rotated about -10 degrees) and Initial phase (white, rotated about -10 degrees). Both arrows are unboxed. The arrows do not reach the bottom of the image.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed photograph with a dark tint on the left half. Title: VisbyCF ExtraBold, all caps, white #FFFFFF, about 6.7% of slide height, roughly 1.1 line spacing. Column headings: VisbyCF Bold, white #FFFFFF, about 3.7% of slide height. Body text: VisbyCF Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 2.6% of slide height, roughly 1.2 line spacing. Accent: purple #9B3CA0 for DISRUPTION and the purple arc. Bullets are small white round dots. Page number: HelveticaNeue, white, about 2.2%. Shapes: no rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Single centred title block sits right of the slide centre, set in five centred lines spanning about x 34 to 92 and y 33 to 72, with a superscript 1 after 'USD 50bn'. A small footnote runs along the bottom right from about x 44 to 97 at y 94, and the page number sits bottom left at about x 2, y 95. Margins are roughly 2 percent of the width at the bottom corners.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of dark grey-green leaves with visible veins, shown in close overhead-style framing with leaves overlapping across the whole slide. Lighting is low-key, with the leaves lit softly from the upper left and the background falling to near black. The photograph is not cropped to a box. Over it are two translucent diagonal shapes: a pale mint panel covering the left 48 percent of the slide at 15 percent opacity, and a purple diagonal band running from the bottom left to the top right at 30 percent opacity, which tints the leaves beneath it. Dominant colours are near black, slate green-grey and muted purple. No logos, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of dark green-grey leaves on near-black, with translucent mint #CBFFED and purple #6C2993 overlays. Dark, moody treatment with a full-bleed leaf photograph under translucent mint and purple diagonal overlays. Title text is white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF Bold, a geometric sans-serif, with no italics or capitals beyond the normal capitals, at about 7.4 percent of slide height for the title lines. Footnote and superscript are white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF Regular and Medium at about 2.6 to 3.0 percent of slide height. Page number is white in HelveticaNeue at about 2.2 percent of slide height. Accents are purple #6C2993 and pale mint #CBFFED. Shapes are plain hard-edged polygons with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light grey field, with the slide title set flush left across the top at about 8% from the top. The left column (heading, rule, subtitle, two bullets) is left-aligned at about x 3%, and the right column (heading, rule, centred statement) sits at about x 66% to 97%, with the two columns separated by the central logo. A footnote runs along the bottom right of centre and the page number sits bottom left at about x 1.7%, y 94.7%.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a daytime sky with cumulus clouds, masked into a large X shape: two diagonal bands run from top-left to bottom-right and from top-right to bottom-left, with the crossing point at the slide centre. The photo is full-bleed within the X mask and not boxed. Dominant colours are deep blue #0B3F8F in the open sky, light blue #3A7CC4 near the edges and white to grey-blue #CAD3DC in the clouds. Lighting is even daylight. Soft grey drop shadows fall along the edges of the X bands into the #DCDFDF field. Centred over the crossing is a white SCBX logo: 'SCB' in a thin geometric sans with a bold white lowercase-style x superscript at upper right. No charts, tables or other graphics.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey #DCDFDF with no gradient. The title is bold black #000000 sans-serif (Visby CF Bold) at about 7.4% of slide height. Section headings are bold purple #6C2993 in all caps at about 4.6%. Subtitle and statement text is bold dark grey #535353 at about 3.7%. Bullets are regular dark grey #535353 Visby CF at about 3.3%, with bullet glyphs in Trebuchet MS. Footnote is regular black #000000 at about 2.6%. Page number is grey #5E5E5E Helvetica Neue at about 2.2%. Two thin dark grey #535353 horizontal rules, about 0.2% of height thick, sit under each heading. The photo has a soft drop shadow along its X edges. No corner radii.

**Layout**

Five equal columns of light translucent panels span the middle of the slide between x 3.3% and x 96.7%, with a 'Gen 1', 'Gen 2' and 'Gen 3' header row above them and a bracket line spanning columns 3 to 5. The title sits top-left beside the SCB X logo at the top margin, left-aligned, with the footer tech-accelerator bar centred at the bottom and the slide number at bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Logos and icons, listed by column. Column 1 (Better Bank): SCB bank app icon, a purple rounded square with a yellow house-like outline and white 'SCB' wordmark with a small asterisk. Column 2 (Digital Lending), top to bottom: CardX wordmark in blue and teal gradient; autoX wordmark in dark blue with a yellow and red X; monix wordmark in dark grey with a teal dot; SCB ABACUS wordmark in red and dark blue with a red circular icon; ALPHA X wordmark in pale grey with a small red X. Column 3 (Digital Ecosystem): Robinhood logo, a purple-to-orange swoosh above the dark grey 'Robinhood' wordmark. Column 4 (Digital Assets and Future Technology), top to bottom: innovest wordmark in purple with a blue and yellow circle and a superscript X; SCB 10X wordmark in purple with a teal and yellow circle and an arc; TOKEN X wordmark in dark grey with a purple and yellow X. Footer: 'Tech Accelerator' text with data X logo (purple and teal swoosh) and TECH X logo (blue, orange, yellow letters with an orange X). No charts, tables or photographs apart from the bottom sea photograph. Logo SCB X at top left is black sans-serif wordmark 'SCB' with a large bold X superscript.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF base with a full-bleed faded photograph of ocean waves across the bottom 39% of the slide. Light, clean corporate slide with a white base and a faded ocean-wave photograph across the bottom third. Titles in purple #6C2993 (Gen headers and column titles) except Climate Tech in green #349000; body text in mid grey #535353 and the Climate Tech body in dark green #0C5F00; the title is black #000000 and the Gen labels are black. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (VisbyCF family): Bold for titles and headings, ExtraBold for Gen labels and footer label, Medium for body. Title text is about 4.3% of slide height, column headings 3.7%, body 2.96%. Panels are white at 70% opacity with a faint soft shadow and square corners. Thin dark grey bracket line over Gen 3. Footer bar is white at 70% opacity with square corners. Dotted black rules run along the bottom at about 95% height.

### Slide 14

**Text**

And this is where / our new journey begins / 14

**Layout**

A single full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide with no grid or columns. The two-line title is centred horizontally in the upper part of the slide, starting at about 12% from the top, and the slide number sits alone at the bottom left with a margin of about 1.7% of the width.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of architecture seen from below. A dark bronze-black curved arch rises from the lower left and lower right and meets at a sharp apex near the top centre of the frame. Two thin polished stainless-steel vertical rods hang from the arch, and a thinner curved stainless cable runs parallel to the arch inside it. Below the apex sits a large pale grey-white curved panel or dome whose top edge forms a rounded peak, and the panel runs off the bottom edge of the frame. Small dark bracket plates attach the rods and cable to the arch. The photo is shot upward against an overcast, evenly lit grey sky with soft diffuse light and no hard shadows. Dominant colours are slate grey sky about #8A8F9A, near-black frame about #222427, and light grey panel about #C9CACD. The image is full-bleed, not boxed, with no visible overlay or tint, and it is cropped at the bottom and sides by the slide edges.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed grey photograph of a sky with architectural detail, sitting over a solid white #FFFFFF fill that covers the slide. Photographic, minimal look. The background is a muted slate grey sky. The title is set in a geometric sans-serif bold (Visby CF Bold), white #FFFFFF, centred, with tight leading, at about 7.4% of slide height. The slide number is in a neutral sans-serif (Helvetica Neue regular), white #FFFFFF, at about 2.2% of slide height. There are no accent colours, no shapes, no lines, no corner radii, no shadows and no gradients on the slide itself.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#8A8F9A]: Full-bleed photograph of a dark curved arch with stainless steel rods and cable above a pale grey curved panel, shot upward against an overcast grey sky (Full-bleed rectangle covering the slide, no crop shape, no overlay or tint, no border, sits above a solid #FFFFFF fill covering the slide.)
- title at x 26.9, y 12.3, w 46.4, h 15.4 [Visby CF Bold, bold weight, sentence case, no italics, no letter-spacing change; font size 7.41% of slide height; #FFFFFF; center aligned]: And this is where / our new journey begins (Two lines, tight leading; line 1 spans x 33.2 to 67.8 and line 2 spans x 26.9 to 73.3; sits over the photograph in the sky area above the arch apex, no fill or shadow.)

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A three-column phase diagram with time ranges and bulleted goals makes a multi-year plan easy to scan.
- Review: The repeated phase graphic (slides 15, 16, 20, 25) works as a progress marker, and slide 7's large stat callouts are easy to read.

**Text**

Our strategy will unfold in 3 phases / PHASE 1: / Build core tech / & data capabilities / (1-2 years) / PHASE 2: / Deliver growth with / scale and synergy / (2-3 years) / PHASE 3: / Monetization and / exponential growth / (3-5 years) / • Build core tech / capabilities and growth / accelerators / • Transform culture and / attract top talent / • Ensure steady earnings / growth from digital / lending businesses… / • …and optimizing and / leaning the bank / • Expand regional / footprints / • Deliver positive EBITDA / for Gen 3 businesses / • Realize value through / IPOs and equity / transactions / • Achieve regional scale / 15

**Layout**

Three equal columns sit side by side beneath a single centred title that spans the top of the slide. Each column has a centred phase label, a centred bold heading with an italic duration line, a thin white horizontal rule, and left-aligned bullet text below. Column centres are about 15%, 50% and 85% of the slide width. The title is centred at about 7.8% from the top. The page number sits bottom left at about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top. The photograph fills the slide below the title band.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph occupying the lower 84.9% of the slide (from y 15.1% to the bottom edge, full width). It is a smooth, matte, rendered 3D abstract of three large curved, ribbon-like loops or tubes in dark slate and blue-grey tones. The central loop is the darkest, around #2B333D, and the outer loops are lighter grey-blue, around #5B636D to #8A9099. Each loop curves upward and away from the viewer, creating overlapping arches. The lighting is soft and diffuse from the upper left, with gentle highlights on the rounded edges. The top band behind the title is a flat warm light grey, #D9D3D2, which blends into the image. Nothing is cropped beyond the edges. Overlays: none beyond the text. Three thin white horizontal rules (lines) sit under each phase heading, each about 0.2% high, drawn in white #FFFFFF at roughly 58.2% from the top.

**Design**

Background is a flat warm light grey #D9D3D2 at the top, transitioning into a rendered grey-slate photograph below. Title is bold purple #6C2993, in a geometric sans-serif (Visby CF Bold), about 6.7% of slide height. Phase labels are white ExtraBold sans-serif (Visby CF ExtraBold) at about 4.4% of slide height, set in capitals. Column headings are white Visby CF Bold at about 3.7%, with the duration line in white Visby CF Regular Oblique at about 3.3%. Bullet text is white Visby CF Regular at about 3.5%, with ragged-right lines and roughly 1.2% space between bullet paragraphs. Bullet glyphs are small white round dots in Trebuchet MS. Page number is white Helvetica Neue at about 2.2%. Rules are thin white lines, about 0.2% high, with no rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: The repeated phase graphic (slides 15, 16, 20, 25) works as a progress marker, and slide 7's large stat callouts are easy to read.

**Text**

Our strategy will unfold in 3 phases
PHASE 1:
Build core tech
& data capabilities
(1-2 years)
Build core tech
capabilities and growth
accelerators
Transform culture and
attract top talent
PHASE 2:
Deliver growth with
scale and synergy
(2-3 years)
Ensure steady earnings
growth from digital
lending businesses...
...and optimizing and
leaning the bank
Expand regional
footprints
PHASE 3:
Monetization and
exponential growth
(3-5 years)
Deliver positive EBITDA
for Gen 3 businesses
Realize value through
IPOs and equity
transactions
Achieve regional scale
16

**Layout**

Three-column layout of equal-width phase columns centred under a centred title at the top, with the photograph filling the lower 85% of the slide behind the columns. Each column stacks a centred PHASE heading, a centred two-line subtitle, a centred italic duration line, a horizontal rule, and left-aligned bullets; the page number sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

One photograph of an abstract 3D sculpture: several large smooth grey curved forms, like twisted or arched ribbon-tubes rising from the bottom edge and sweeping up and across the slide, with soft matte shading. It is full-bleed across the full width and starts at 15.1% from the top, running to the bottom edge with no border. Lighting is soft and diffuse from the upper left; dominant colours are mid slate grey (about #6E7782), darker blue-grey shadows (about #4B5560) and pale mauve-grey background (about #D9D3D2). A translucent #D8D3D2 overlay at 50% opacity covers the photo from x 21.2% to the right edge and from y 17.4% to the bottom, so the Phase 2 and Phase 3 columns appear dimmed and the Phase 1 column on the left is not overlaid. Three thin white horizontal rules sit under each phase subtitle block.

**Design**

Background is a pale warm grey-mauve (#D9D3D2) with a full-bleed grey sculptural photograph across the lower part. Title is purple (#6C2993) VisbyCF-Bold, sans-serif, about 6.7% of slide height, centred. Phase headings are VisbyCF-ExtraBold in white (#FFFFFF), about 4.4% of slide height, with the right two dimmed by the overlay. Subtitles are VisbyCF-Bold white, about 3.7% of height, durations are VisbyCF-RegularOblique white about 3.3%, bullets are VisbyCF-Regular white about 3.5%, bullet glyphs are TrebuchetMS white. Rules are white hairlines. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on text or rules; the photograph itself carries soft shading.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph with a semi-transparent white overlay. The title is centred in a band across the top at about 9.6% from the top. A vertical white divider at the horizontal centre splits the body into two columns: the left column holds a large purple '3' with the label 'Group Centers of Excellence' above three white icons with captions; the right column holds a large purple '2' with the label 'Growth accelerators' above two logos with captions. Margins are roughly 4% to 8% on the sides. The slide number sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left and 94.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a minimalist white architectural courtyard with large angled concrete slabs, a stepped platform in the lower centre and a flat grey ground plane, under a blue-grey sky that fades to pale grey near the horizon. Full-bleed, not cropped, with a white semi-transparent overlay that lightens and softens it. Dominant colours are light grey, blue-grey and white.
Icons (white outline line icons): fingerprint (Data & AI), cloud (Cloud), padlock with keyhole (Cyber).
Logos: TECHX wordmark with a multicolour letterset (blue T, purple E, yellow-orange H, cyan C) and a small multicolour X superscript; datax wordmark with dark grey 'data' followed by a purple-magenta whale-tail swoosh forming the X.
No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background is a photograph of grey architecture under a blue-grey sky, with a white semi-transparent overlay. Title is purple #6C2993, VisbyCF Bold, about 5.2% of slide height. Large numerals are purple #6C2993 in VisbyCF ExtraBold, about 12.2% of slide height. Labels and captions are white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF Bold (labels, about 5.2%) and VisbyCF Medium (captions, about 4.3%). Sans-serif geometric typeface, no italics, no capitals or letter-spacing changes. Divider is a thin solid white line. Icons are white outlines with no fill. No shadows, corner radii or gradients on shapes.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the slide behind a light grey field, with a large X-shaped overlay forming the central motif. Two text columns sit left and right of the X: the DataX block left-aligned at x 3.7%, the TechX block right-aligned to about x 96%, each with a bold headline above a smaller body paragraph. The DataX logo sits top-left, the TechX logo top-right, and the slide number sits bottom-left at about x 1.7%, y 94.7%; there is no title placeholder and no footer.

**Visuals**

A photograph of a modern glass-walled meeting room: several people in business clothing sit and stand around a long dark conference table, seen from behind and from the side; a standing man in a grey sweater at the centre, a seated woman in a beige top at left, a seated person in a dark suit at right with a laptop on the table, and a person in a purple top partly visible at centre-right. Background shows blurred office windows and ceiling lights in cool white and pale blue. Lighting is soft, diffuse daylight with bright highlights in the upper glass. Composition is wide with the table running horizontally across the lower half. The photograph is full-bleed and is overlaid by a large light grey (#DCDFDF) X shape: two grey diagonal bands cross the slide, masking the photo into four triangular areas, with the left and right triangles fully grey. A translucent purple (#6C2993) diagonal band runs from top-right to bottom-left across the X, overlapping the photo. Small grey ellipsis '…' sits at centre-right, about x 60.9%, y 40.8%. Logos: DataX logo (dark grey 'data' wordmark with a stylised purple-magenta whale in a rounded arc) at top-left; TechX logo (multicolour rounded letters 'TECH' in blue, orange, green, teal, purple with a small colourful X mark at the top-right of the H) at top-right.

**Design**

Background light grey #DCDFDF. Headlines in VisbyCF-Bold, purple #6C2993, about 4.4% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%), left-aligned on the DataX side and right-aligned on the TechX side, sentence case, line spacing about 1.3 times size. Body text in VisbyCF-Medium, dark grey #535353, about 3.3% of slide height, sentence case, tight leading. Slide number in HelveticaNeue, grey #5E5E5E, about 2.2%. Accents are purple #6C2993 in the X band and headlines. Shapes are flat with no visible shadows or rounded corners; the X bands are straight diagonal edges. The purple band appears semi-transparent over the photograph.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: The repeated phase graphic (slides 15, 16, 20, 25) works as a progress marker, and slide 7's large stat callouts are easy to read.

**Text**

Our strategy will unfold in 3 phases / PHASE 1: / Build core tech / & data capabilities / (1-2 years) / • Build core tech / capabilities and growth / accelerators / • Transform culture and / attract top talent / PHASE 2: / Deliver growth with / scale and synergy / (2-3 years) / • Ensure steady earnings / growth from digital / lending businesses… / • …and optimizing and / leaning the bank / • Expand regional / footprints / PHASE 3: / Monetization and / exponential growth / (3-5 years) / • Deliver positive EBITDA / for Gen 3 businesses / • Realize value through / IPOs and equity / transactions / • Achieve regional scale / 20

**Layout**

Single full-bleed slide with a centred title across the top band, three equal-width columns below it (left column centred at about x 20%, middle at about x 50%, right at about x 80%), each holding a phase heading, a subheading with years in italics, a thin horizontal rule and a bullet list. The title is centred at y 7.8% to 14.5%. Column headings start at y 37.4%, bullets end near y 96%. Left and right columns sit under 50% tinted overlays; the middle column is unobscured. Page number '20' sits bottom-left at x 1.7%, y 94.7%.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed behind all text: a soft-focus 3D render of sculpted grey ribbon-like forms curving upward and arching across the slide. Subject: three smooth curved bands; the central band is a dark slate-blue (about #2B323B) band rising from the bottom edge and arcing over the top, flanked by lighter grey bands on the left and right that are partly hidden under translucent tints. Composition: the bands fill the lower two-thirds and cross the full width, cropped at the top and bottom edges; no people, no text in the image. Lighting: diffuse, even studio light with soft gradient highlights on the grey bands and a darker shadowed interior of the centre band. Dominant colours: warm light grey #D9D3D2, mid grey, slate #2B323B. Overlays: two rectangles filled #D9D3D2 at 50% opacity covering the left column (x 0 to 38.2%, y 23.3% to bottom) and right column (x 58.4 to 100%, y 16.9% to bottom), which wash out the flanking bands and the text placed over them. A base rectangle filled #D9D3D2 at 100% sits under the photo. Thin rules: white horizontal lines about 0.3% of slide height thick at y 58.1% under each column heading, left from x 4.8 to 28.6%, middle from x 40.0 to 63.9%, right from x 72.4 to 96.3%.

**Design**

Background is warm light grey #D9D3D2 with a 3D grey and slate photograph covering the lower area. Title is purple #6C2993, bold geometric sans-serif (VisbyCF-Bold), about 6.7% of slide height. Phase headings are white (#FFFFFF) extra-bold geometric sans (VisbyCF-ExtraBold) at about 4.4% of slide height, appearing grey on the tinted columns. Subheadings are white bold sans at about 3.7%, years are white regular oblique at about 3.3%. Bullet body text is white regular geometric sans (VisbyCF-Regular) at about 3.5%, with bullets in a TrebuchetMS dot. Page number is white HelveticaNeue at about 2.2%. Rules are thin white lines. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on text; the photo has soft natural shading.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.
- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.
- What would change the score: Replace decorative photos behind data-bearing slides with simple charts or tables for the targets on slides 21 and 24, and trim the many mood-only interstitial slides.

**Text**

Digital lenders on track / to deliver strong / financial results
Higher, double-digit margins
Growing profit contribution, / with higher ROE
Equity transactions
• Successful VC Fund-raising
• VC Fund-raising
• IPO 2025
• IPO 2027
21

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed photo. The title sits top-left at about 10% from the top and 6% from the left, stacked over three lines. The left column holds four logos stacked vertically at about 34% to 67% height. The right column holds three icon-and-text rows (about 25% to 60% height) above a bulleted list of four items (about 67% to 92% height) with logos to the right of the bullet text, all left-aligned. The page number sits bottom-left at about 95% height and 2% from the left margin.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a dark brown-burgundy leather wallet, open and angled, lying in the left and upper portion of the frame with a white stitched edge running diagonally from top-right to bottom-left. Pale banknotes (grey-beige with brown and purple print, showing numerals such as 1283042 and 3986) fan out from the wallet toward the bottom-right. The photo is full-bleed and covers the whole slide under a light translucent tint. Lighting is soft and even, with a neutral grey-white cast. Dominant colours are burgundy, grey-beige and pale grey. Three purple line icons: a banknote icon (rectangle with a central circle and two dots) at about 46.6% x, 25.3% y, 7.5% by 5.4%; a rising line chart icon (L-shaped axes with an upward arrow trend line) at about 47.7% x, 38.3% y, 5.1% by 9.0%; a bank building icon (pediment, four columns and base) at about 47.8% x, 51.7% y, 5.1% by 9.0%. Logos: CardX (blue-to-teal gradient wordmark), autoX (white wordmark with a yellow and red X on the left slide, blue wordmark with yellow and red X on the bullet line), monix (grey wordmark with a teal circle as the o), and SCB ABACUS (red circle with yellow dot icon, purple SCB and red ABACUS wordmark). Left column logos: CardX at about 5.8% x, 34.2% y, 9.5% by 5.8%; autoX at about 5.5% x, 42.7% y, 11.8% by 6.8%; monix at about 6.3% x, 54.5% y, 10.8% by 3.4%; SCB ABACUS at about 5.7% x, 61.6% y, 14.9% by 7.3%. Bullet-line logos: SCB ABACUS at about 81.2% x, 67.4% y, 12.2% by 4.8%; monix at about 69.1% x, 75.2% y, 9.6% by 3.0%; CardX at about 59.7% x, 74.3% y, 13.1% by 23.4% (bounding box as measured, visible logo about 61.8% to 70.5% x, 80.9% to 84.9% y); SCB ABACUS at about 72.7% x, 80.8% y, 12.2% by 4.8%; autoX at about 58.4% x, 87.4% y, 16.0% by 12.6% (visible logo about 62% to 73% x, 88% to 92% y).

**Design**

Background: Photograph of a wallet and banknotes under a light #FBF4EF tint covering the full slide. Full-bleed wallet photograph under a light translucent off-white tint (#FBF4EF) that washes out the image. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) Visby Bold sans-serif, about 5.6% of slide height per line, left-aligned. Right-column statements are Visby Bold in deep purple #551D6D at about 5.2% of slide height, left-aligned. Icons are solid purple #551D6D. Bullet text is white (#FFFFFF) Visby Medium sans-serif at about 4.4% of slide height, with bullet dots at about 8.9% height in white. Page number is Helvetica Neue regular in white at about 2.2% of slide height. Line spacing is about 6.7% of slide height between title lines and about 7% between bullet lines. No shapes, rules, corner radii or shadows; the logos are plain raster images.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.
- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.

**Text**

Building competitive edge / through technology
From customer / acquisition…
… to credit risk / control
Our proprietary / technology includes / AI-based credit scoring / & collection models
Controlling credit costs, / while serving / the underserved
Our digital lenders offer / a convenient e2e digital / journey
Keeping customer / acquisition cost / down
22

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light grey field with a large X-shaped photograph behind it. The centred purple title sits across the top, spanning about 27% to 74% of the width. The left column (about 3% to 28% wide) holds the headline 'From customer acquisition…', three phone mockups, and body text, all left-aligned. The right column (about 73% to 96.5% wide) holds the headline '… to credit risk control', a 2x2 logo grid, and right-aligned body text. Left and right margins are about 3.3%. The slide number '22' sits at the bottom left, about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top. There is no footer or logo bar.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph of two hands, one holding a smartphone and the other touching its screen, shown in greyscale with dark charcoal and slate tones. It is cropped and masked into a large X-shaped band that runs diagonally from the top corners to the bottom corners, with its visible edges running across the full slide height. The light grey background shows through the triangular notches at the top centre, left and right edges, and bottom centre. The photo is not tinted, and the title sits over the upper part of the X. 2) Three smartphone mockups side by side in the left column, each with a black rounded bezel and a notch, roughly 7.5% of slide width each and overlapping slightly. Phone 1 (left): deep navy screen with a teal and white gradient glow at the bottom right and a white lightning-bolt icon in a rounded indigo square (#3B3B9A approx). Phone 2 (centre): royal blue top fading to orange at the bottom, with the white 'FINNIX' wordmark in red-orange capitals (the 'N' and 'I' styled as slashes) and a photo strip of five smiling young people in colourful casual clothes. Phone 3 (right): bright yellow screen with yellow sunburst rays, a cartoon yellow character with a blue cap laughing and holding a phone, a blue rectangular button with white Thai text, and a small Thai caption above. 3) A yellow rectangle (#F0CF23) sits behind phone 3 at about 20.8% to 28.4% horizontally and 37.3% to 62.7% vertically. 4) A black shadow at 28% opacity sits under the navy app icon. 5) Logos in the right column, listed in reading order: SCB ABACUS (red circular 'O' emblem with a yellow-and-blue dot, then 'SCB' in purple and 'ABACUS' in orange-red); monix (grey lowercase wordmark with a teal 'o' letter); CardX (teal and light-blue lowercase wordmark with a gradient); autoX (bold royal-blue lowercase 'auto' followed by a red-and-yellow 'X').

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #DCDFDF, with a charcoal greyscale photograph of hands and a phone in an X-shaped band across the slide. Light grey background (#DCDFDF) with a charcoal greyscale photo in an X shape. Title in bold purple (#6C2993). Headlines in bold dark grey (#535353). Body in medium-weight dark grey (#535353). Accent yellow #F0CF23 behind one phone. Typeface is Visby CF, a geometric sans-serif, bold for the title and headlines and medium for body text. Title is about 6.7% of slide height; headlines about 5.2%; body about 3.7%. Slide number is in Helvetica Neue, regular, about 2.2%, in #5E5E5E. Phone mockups have rounded corners and black bezels. Soft drop shadow under the navy app icon. No visible shape outlines.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.

During Phase 2, we will also lay the foundation / for our regional expansion / To expand / customer base via / a regional financial / platform / To expand regional / footprints through / strategic / partnerships / To expand to CLMV / once domestic / leadership / solidified / Poised to secure / digital asset / infrastructure / provider position in / the region / To build a / regional hub to / attract talent and / invest globally / 23

**Layout**

Five equal-width tall panels sit in a single row across the lower half of the slide, each about 19.4% wide with about 1.2% gutters, starting at 0.8% from the left edge and ending at 99.2%. The panels run from y 41.9% to y 95.9%. A centred two-line title sits in the upper third, starting at y 17.6%. Each panel holds a centred logo above a centred three- to five-line caption. The slide number sits bottom-left at x 1.7%, y 94.7%.

**Visuals**

Five translucent rectangular panels, each with a thin white outline and a faint light tint, showing the skyline photograph through them. Each panel holds one logo: CardX wordmark in blue-to-cyan gradient letters, Robinhood logo with a purple, magenta and orange swoosh above black wordmark with a location-pin O, autoX wordmark in white with a yellow and red X, innovestX wordmark in purple with a blue and yellow circular O and a small purple x, and SCB10X logo in purple with a grey I, teal O and yellow X. Behind the panels is a full-bleed photograph of a heavy grey cloudy sky over a hazy city skyline on a waterfront, with tall towers and low apartment blocks at the bottom and a dark grey strip along the bottom edge. The photograph is tinted grey and slightly darkened, so the purple title and white captions stand out.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed grey-toned photograph: dark slate sky #4A5560 at top fading to light grey #9AA0A6 near the horizon, with a dark grey base #3A3F45 at the bottom. Title is set in a geometric sans-serif bold (Visby-style) in deep purple #551D6D, centred. Captions are set in a geometric sans-serif medium (Visby-style) in white #FFFFFF, centred. The page number is in Helvetica Neue-style regular in white #FFFFFF. Title text is about 6.7% of slide height; caption text is about 3.3% of slide height; page number about 2.2%. Panels have thin white outlines with square corners and no visible shadows.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Target metrics are set beside the strategic levers that drive them, which ties goals directly to actions.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.
- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.
- What would change the score: Replace decorative photos behind data-bearing slides with simple charts or tables for the targets on slides 21 and 24, and trim the many mood-only interstitial slides.

**Text**

Our Better Bank Strategy / Low Teens / ROE by 2025 / Mid-to-high / 30s / Cost-to-income by 2025 / Accelerate / digitization / journey / A more complete digital / banking offering / Better quality / loan book / Tighten risk-return and / optimize our RWA 1 / Higher fee income / Focus on / bancassurance and / wealth management / 1 RWA - Risk Weighted Assets / 24

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the slide with the SCB logo at top left and the title beside it on the upper band. Stat block with two white icons sits in the upper right, starting at about x 65 percent. Three pillar columns sit in the lower third, separated by two thin white vertical rules at about x 36.5 and 66.2 percent, and run from about y 68 to 91 percent. The footnote is right-aligned at the bottom right, and the page number '24' sits at bottom left, about 1.7 percent from the left edge. Left margin of the title is about 14.5 percent; pillar headings are left-aligned at x 10.3, 40.0 and 69.6 percent.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of an abstract architectural sculpture: undulating, stacked curved ribbons of dark grey and off-white surfaces that sweep from the lower left up and across to the right, set above a flat pale grey concrete floor. Composition is horizontal with the ribbons occupying the middle band, sky grey at top. Lighting is soft and diffuse, with no harsh shadows. Dominant colours are mid grey #7A7F84, charcoal #2E2F31 and off-white #D9DBDC. Not cropped; full-bleed; no overlay other than a slight grey tint. Icon 1: white bar chart icon, an L-shaped axis with four ascending vertical bars, outline-free solid white (#FFFFFF), positioned to the left of 'Low Teens'. Icon 2: white scissors icon, open blades with two ring handles, solid white (#FFFFFF), positioned to the left of 'Mid-to-high 30s'. Logo: SCB bank logo, a rounded purple square with a gold (#F5B400) stylised house or pointed-arch emblem above white 'SCB' letters and a tiny multicolour dot at the lower right.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed grey photograph with a light grey tint. Titles and key stats: white (#FFFFFF) for the stat headlines 'Low Teens' and 'Mid-to-high 30s'; purple #551D6D for the slide title and the three pillar headings. Sub-captions 'ROE by 2025' and 'Cost-to-income by 2025' in dark grey #535353. Body text in black #000000. Accent: purple #551D6D, gold #F5B400 within the logo. Typeface: Visby CF, a geometric rounded sans-serif; ExtraBold for the title and stat headlines, Bold for pillar headings, DemiBold for stat captions, Medium for body, Regular for footnote. Slide title about 6.7 percent of slide height; pillar headings about 4.6 percent; body text about 3.7 percent; footnote about 2.6 percent. Lines and shapes: two thin white vertical rules, about 0.2 percent wide. No corner radii except the rounded logo square, no shadows, no gradients on shapes.

**Layout**

Three equal columns of phase blocks sit below a centred title at the top, with the title centred horizontally at about 7.8% from the top. Each column has a centred header (PHASE n:, a bold subhead and an italic time range), a thin horizontal rule, and left-aligned bullets, with the columns starting at roughly 8%, 40% and 72% of slide width. The slide number 25 sits at the bottom-left corner about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph-style 3D render across the whole slide (image box 0,15.1 to 100,100) showing three smooth, matte grey curved ribbons or arches, sweeping from left to right and rising in height. The left ribbon is pale light grey, the middle is mid grey and the right is dark slate blue-grey (about #3A4048 at its darkest). Soft diffuse studio lighting with gentle highlights on the ribbon edges. Light beige-grey background at the top (about #D9D3D2). The image carries a light whitish overlay that washes out the left and middle ribbons, so Phase 1 and Phase 2 text appears greyed while Phase 3 stays fully white. No charts, icons or logos.

**Design**

Background is a light warm grey #D9D3D2 with a faded 3D ribbon render in the lower two-thirds. Title is purple #6C2993 in a bold geometric sans-serif (Visby CF Bold) at about 6.7% of slide height, sitting over the light top band. Phase headers are extra-bold white (#FFFFFF, appearing greyed by overlay for Phases 1 and 2) at about 4.4% of slide height. Phase subheads are bold white at about 3.7%, time ranges are regular italic at about 3.3%, bullets are regular white at about 3.5% with a Trebuchet MS bullet glyph. Divider rules are thin white lines of about 1 px. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients on text; the 3D ribbons carry soft shading. Page number is Helvetica Neue at about 2.2% of slide height.

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.

**Layout**

Three centred columns of equal width separated by two thin vertical white rules, each column holding a bold purple headline above a white body block; the headlines start at about 15 percent of the slide height and the body blocks sit below them. The slide number 26 sits at the bottom-left margin, and there is no header or footer band.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed black-and-white photograph of the bow of a white ship seen from the deck: a tall vertical mast runs up the centre, a bell hangs at the centre-bottom, a dark railing crosses the lower foreground, and open sea with a horizon at about 58 percent of the height sits beneath a flat grey overcast sky. The composition is centred on the mast with the bow curving out to both lower corners; lighting is diffuse and even; dominant colours are mid to dark grey, white and charcoal. The photograph is uncropped and covered by a dark grey tint that lowers its brightness behind the text. Two thin white vertical divider lines, each about 0.2 percent of the slide width, run from about 9.7 percent to 52 percent of the slide height at x about 34.4 percent and x about 66.2 percent.

**Design**

Background is a darkened grayscale photograph with a charcoal tint. Headlines are bold purple #551D6D in a geometric sans-serif (Visby CF Bold), about 5.6 percent of slide height. Body text is white #FFFFFF in Visby CF DemiBold, about 3.7 percent of slide height, centred with tight line spacing. Accent is purple #551D6D for headings and white #FFFFFF for dividers. Divider lines are white, about 1 pixel thick, with no corner radius, shadow or gradient. The slide number is shown in charcoal #535353 Visby CF DemiBold and a second white HelveticaNeue copy.

### Slide 27

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: many slides put text directly on busy photos with weak contrast (slides 8, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27), tiny low-contrast text on slides 10, 23 and 30, and the Better Bank and digital lender targets (slides 21, 24) are shown as labels with little chart or numeric support.

**Text**

Our focus is
to provide
a digital asset
infrastructure
We are building infrastructure
with a focus on underlying
technology…
…and not to pursue speculative
investments with no commercial
use cases
27

**Layout**

Two-zone split layout: a large X-shaped photograph fills the left side and bleeds off the top, bottom and left edges, while the right side is open light grey. The title is centred inside the left part of the X, and the body statement is centred at about 75% of slide width in the right zone, both vertically centred around the middle of the slide. There is no header or footer; the slide number sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top.

**Visuals**

One photograph, cropped into a large X shape: two diagonal bands cross at centre-left, bleeding off all four slide edges. The photo shows blurred, dense ornate Gothic arch tracery (pointed arches, trefoil rosettes and vertical ribs) in a blue-grey, slate and muted sand palette, with a soft blur and a faint halftone texture. The title text sits over the central and lower-left part of the X. Lighting is flat and even; dominant colours are slate blue-grey about #4A5A66, pale sage about #A9B8B6 and sand about #C8BFA5. A soft grey drop shadow runs along the edges of the X where it meets the light background. No charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is flat light grey #DCDFDF. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Visby CF ExtraBold (geometric sans-serif, bold), set in four centred lines at about 6.67% of slide height with line pitch about 7.2%. Body text is dark grey #535353 in Visby CF, mixing DemiBold for plain words and ExtraBold for emphasised words, at about 4.81% of slide height with line pitch about 5.8%, centre-aligned. Slide number is white in Helvetica Neue regular at about 2.22% of slide height. Shape style: the X is a photographic clip with sharp straight edges, no rounded corners, soft drop shadow, no border. No gradients on the text.

### Slide 28

**Text**

We have a structure to capture / growth in digital assets as / technologies mature / TOKENX / Primary market / Monetization / Offer digital asset / trading, investment / and infrastructure / services / Blockchain / & Digital / asset cluster / SCB 10X / Knowledge and / capability center / Acquire capabilities / through venture / investing and / incubation / More digital assets / & infrastructure / innovestX / Secondary market / 28

**Layout**

Full-bleed 3D render of a stepped arrangement of grey blocks fills the slide. The title sits top-left in three left-aligned lines starting at about 6% from the left edge and 8.5% from the top. Labels are placed around the blocks: left-aligned Monetization text at the upper right, right-aligned text at the left, and centred text on the centre and bottom blocks. The slide number sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top. There is no footer other than the slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style 3D render of five light grey-blue blocks arranged as a stepped staircase, viewed from a high front-left angle. The top block at upper centre carries the TOKENX logo with the caption Primary market. The left middle block carries the SCB 10X logo. The right block carries the innovestX logo with the caption Secondary market. The centre block sits between them with the label Blockchain & Digital asset cluster. The front bottom block carries the label More digital assets & infrastructure. Lighting is soft and diffuse from above, with gentle shadows on the block sides. Dominant colours are slate grey-blue (#8E9DA8 to #B9C6CE) with a darker shadow tone at the left. The image covers the whole slide and is not cropped. Two thin white leader lines connect the left and right text callouts to the left and right blocks. No overlay or tint is visible beyond the background fill.

**Design**

Background is a soft grey-blue gradient with a lighter area at the lower right. Titles and body text are white (#FFFFFF). Accent colours come from the logos: purple #5B2A86, teal #1FA6B8 and yellow #F2E53A for SCB 10X, and grey #4D4D4D with a teal and purple X for TOKENX. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Visby CF, Bold for titles and headings and Medium for body text. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, headings about 4.4%, body about 3.7%, and the slide number about 2.2%. Leader lines are thin white strokes of about 1 px. Blocks have softly rounded edges. No corner radii, shadows or gradients are applied to text.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Bold Vision With Prudent Execution
And Discipline
29

**Layout**

Single centred composition over a full-bleed photograph. The SCB X logo sits centred above the title at about 33% from the top; the two-line title is centred horizontally across the middle of the slide, with the second line centred beneath the first. The slide number sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left edge and 94.7% from the top. No footer or other furniture.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed black-and-white photograph (desaturated, dark grey tones) of a ship's chrome steering wheel viewed from above. A person's gloved or bare hands grip the spoke at centre; a forearm in a dark rust/brown sleeve with a metal watch or bracelet runs up from the bottom centre. On the right, a dark domed binnacle or compass housing with a graduated dial is visible; on the left, the edge of a person's head and shoulder is cropped. The photo is darkened by a dark grey overlay (about 50 to 60% opacity, near #1E1F23) so the white text reads clearly. Lighting is soft, from the upper left. Composition is close-up, slightly high angle, cropped at all edges, full-bleed. No other graphics beyond the SCB X logo, a white wordmark with a superscript bold white X, placed at about x 41.9%, y 33.3%, w 16.2%, h 17.8%.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed darkened grey photograph (dominant tones near #2A2C30 to #5A5D63). Title and logo are white (#FFFFFF). Title typeface is VisbyCF Bold, a geometric sans-serif, roughly 7.4% of slide height per line (capitals about 5.2% of slide height), line spacing about 7.8% of slide height baseline to baseline. Slide number is HelveticaNeue regular, white, about 2.2% of slide height. No borders, shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

- Suggested repair: Put text on solid or darkened panels and enlarge the small body text on slides 8, 10, 21, 22, 23, 24 and 30, and fix the 'Hight' typo on slides 7 and 8.

**Layout**

Three equal columns sit under a single centred title near the top, separated by two thin white vertical rules. Each column holds a purple icon, a purple bold heading and centred white body text, with the slide number in the bottom-left corner.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed architectural photograph behind everything: a grey glass-panelled building facade curving up from the lower right edge, with diagonal mullion lines, under a pale grey-blue sky over a flat grey plaza floor with a dark stepped edge at the right. The whole photo is overlaid with a light grey tint (#DCDFDF). Three flat purple (#551D6D) solid icons: a compass rose (eight-point star inside a ring, centred above Investment, x 13.3 y 31.0 w 7.6 h 13.6); a police-style cap with a shield badge (centred above Compliance, x 46.2 y 32.8 w 7.6 h 9.8); a classical building with a pediment and four columns on a stepped base (centred above Governance, x 79.7 y 33.4 w 6.2 h 11.0). Two thin white vertical divider lines: one at about x 34.5 from y 33.2 to y 86.8, one at about x 66.4 from y 33.2 to y 86.8.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed architectural photograph (grey glass building and plaza under pale grey-blue sky) with a #DCDFDF light grey tint overlay. Full-bleed photographic background with a light grey #DCDFDF tint over it, giving a muted grey-blue look. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF; column headings and icons are purple #551D6D. Typeface is Visby CF (geometric humanist sans-serif): ExtraBold for the title and headings, Medium for body. Title is about 6.7% of slide height, headings about 5.6%, body about 3.7%. Divider lines are thin white strokes. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on shapes. Page number is in Helvetica Neue regular.

Photograph of a white lighthouse with a red lantern room standing on a rocky headland, with a bright beam of light extending horizontally to the right across the image. Stormy sea with large white-crested waves breaks against the rocks at the bottom; the sky is heavy dark grey clouds at top. Lighting is low-key with the lighthouse lamp as the brightest point. Composition is full-bleed, uncropped, with the lighthouse placed left of centre and the beam leading toward the text. Dominant colours are slate blue and dark grey in the sky, white and rust-brown on the rocks, and deep teal-grey in the water. A large semi-transparent dark grey X-shaped graphic overlay (two intersecting diagonal bands forming large triangles) tints the left and right portions of the image.

Body is VisbyCF Medium, about 4.4% of slide height, with generous line spacing between bullets. Bullets are small solid white dots. Page number is HelveticaNeue at 2.2% of slide height. **Visuals**

One photograph on the right side of the slide, placed full height (y 0 to 100%) from x about 51% to the right edge, with a second placed image of the same scene at x 51.6%, also full height. Subject: a hand of a person in a grey suit with white shirt cuff reaching upward toward a golden trophy cup, seen from below against a bright sky. Action: the hand is raising or touching the trophy, which is backlit by warm sunlight creating a glow. Composition: low-angle, tightly framed, cropped at the top and right edges, the trophy in the upper-middle of the picture and the arm running diagonally from lower left to right. Lighting: warm, bright sunlight with orange-gold highlights on the trophy and pale blue-grey sky. Dominant colours: #DCDFDF light grey, warm peach-gold highlights, grey suit tones and skin tones. The photo is not boxed; it is cropped to the slide edge and has a light grey translucent chevron overlay shaped like a large X or folded V that runs from top centre down to the lower right. A solid purple band #6C2993 runs diagonally from top right to bottom right behind and across the photo, partly visible through it, at x 70.3 to 100%, full height, with a light purple diagonal stripe crossing the lower right. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #DCDFDF. Light grey background #DCDFDF with no gradient. Title text in deep purple #561C6D, bold geometric sans-serif (VisbyCF-Bold or closest: geometric sans-serif, bold), about 6.7% of slide height per line with tight leading. Body text is black #000000 in VisbyCF Regular geometric sans-serif, about 2.8% of slide height, with bold lead-in names in VisbyCF Bold. Superscript 'th' characters are about 1.9% of slide height. Page number is grey #5E5E5E in Helvetica Neue regular, about 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour is purple #6C2993 in the diagonal band; the chevron overlay is light grey semi-transparent. No corner radii, no shadows on text; the diagonal shapes have a soft edge shadow where they overlap the photo.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Why SCBX?
We pursue GROWTH opportunities with
greater purposes.
We are ADVANTAGED with a solid customer
base and brand as a launchpad for early win.
We put in place a structure to orchestrate
group-wide SYNERGIES.
We offer a BALANCED GROWTH profile while
making new investments with DISCIPLINE.
We maximize CAPITAL EFFICIENCY in a way
banks cannot.
33

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a full-bleed photograph: the title Why SCBX? sits top-left at about 16% from the top, and a left-aligned body column of five paragraphs starts at about 46% from the left and runs down to about 88% of the height. Paragraphs are separated by blank line gaps. The slide number 33 sits bottom-left at about 1.7% from the left and 94.7% from the top. The left margin of the title is about 13% and the right text edge stays under about 92%.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed black-and-white photograph of a ship's bow or deck seen from above-left: a curved white metal railing with white triangular stanchions and dark cylindrical bollards in the lower left, and a dense explosion of white sea spray and dark water filling the centre and right. Lighting is harsh and high-contrast, with the spray brightly lit against a near-black sea at upper right. The composition is wide and full-bleed, uncropped at the slide edges. A translucent large light-grey chevron or V shape sits in the upper left, and a diagonal purple band runs from top centre down to the lower left edge, overlapping the photo and the title. Dominant colours are greyscale (#FFFFFF highlights, mid grey #808080, near black #111111) and purple #411959.

**Design**

Background is a grayscale photograph with a dark overlay on the left portion. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF. Accents are purple #411959 in the diagonal band and the chevron. Typefaces: title is Visby CF ExtraBold, a geometric rounded-terminal sans-serif, at about 7.4% of slide height. Body is Visby CF Medium, geometric sans-serif, at about 3.9% of slide height, with key words set in Visby CF Bold. The slide number is Helvetica Neue regular at about 2.2% of slide height. Line spacing is about 1.2 times the font size. No corner radii, no shadows on text. The diagonal band is a flat fill with about 50% opacity.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Above & Beyond with / SCBX

**Layout**

Full-bleed black-and-white photograph covering the whole slide with a purple diagonal band and a large translucent chevron overlay at top left. The title sits right of centre in the upper third, with the SCBX logo stacked beneath it, right-aligned to the same block; the slide number sits bottom left at the margin.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed greyscale photograph of a ship's bow or deck edge in heavy seas: a large white-grey wave crest bursts up across the centre and right, spray hangs in the air, dark ocean fills the upper right corner. In the lower left, the foreground shows a curved white railing with angled white panels, black bollards on a dark deck, and a black spotlight on a post. Lighting is flat and overcast with high contrast in the dark sea area. Dominant colours are greys and near-black with a purple diagonal band. The photograph is cropped to the slide edges, with a dark translucent black overlay (about 30 percent opacity) on the left portion and a purple #411959 overlay at about 50 percent opacity over the same left portion, forming a diagonal band from top right to bottom left. A large translucent grey chevron shape (pointing down) sits at top left over the photograph. The SCBX logo is a white wordmark 'SCB' in a thin geometric sans with a bold white 'X' superscript, at upper right beneath the title.

**Design**

Background is a greyscale photograph with dark overlays. Title is white bold geometric sans (VisbyCF-Bold style), about 7.4 percent of slide height. Slide number is white regular Helvetica-style sans at about 2.2 percent of slide height. Purple band #411959 at 50 percent opacity with a black 30 percent overlay on the left 47 percent of the slide. No corner radii, no shadows, flat fills.
